(eTLD+1)\n//   - Valid outputs have labels ≤63 characters\n//   - Valid outputs have total length ≤253 characters\nfunc FuzzValidateDomain(f *testing.F) {\n\t// Seed corpus: 9 diverse inputs covering valid and invalid cases\n\tseeds := []string{\n\t\t\"example.com\",           // #1: Simple valid domain\n\t\t\"EXAMPLE.COM\",           // #2: Uppercase (normalizes)\n\t\t\"www.example.co.uk\",     // #3: Multi-level TLD with subdomain\n\t\t\"münchen.de\",            // #4: IDN input (converts to punycode)\n\t\t\"\",                      // #5: Empty string\n\t\t\"example..com\",          // #6: Empty label\n\t\t\"-example.com\",          // #7: Leading hyphen\n\t\t\"http://example.com\",    // #8: URL scheme\n\t\tstrings.Repeat(\"a\", 64) + \".com\", // #9: Label too long (64 chars)\n\t}\n\tfor _, seed := range seeds {\n\t\tf.Add(seed)\n\t}\n\n\tf.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, input string) {\n\t\t// Property 1: Must never panic (the test framework handles this)\n\t\tresult, err := Parse(input)\n\n\t\t// If parsing succeeded, verify invariants\n\t\tif err == nil && result != nil {\n\t\t\t// Property 2: Valid domains contain at least one dot\n\t\t\tif !strings.Contains(result.Domain, \".\") {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"valid domain missing dot: %q\", result.Domain)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Property 3: Each label ≤63 characters\n\t\t\tlabels := strings.Split(result.Domain, \".\")\n\t\t\tfor i, label := range labels {\n\t\t\t\tif len(label) > 63 {\n\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"label %d exceeds 63 chars (%d): %q\", i+1, len(label), label)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Property 4: Total domain length ≤253 characters\n\t\t\tif len(result.Domain) > 253 {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"domain exceeds 253 chars (%d): %q\", len(result.Domain), result.Domain)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\nfunc TestParse_EffectiveTLDPlusOne(t *testing.T) {\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname  string\n\t\tinput string\n\t\twant  string\n\t}{\n\t\t{name: \"com\", the full Check flow with a mock server.\nfunc TestRDAPClientCheck(t *testing.T) {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tt.Run(\"registered domain\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, \"/domain/example.com\") {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"unexpected path: %s\", r.URL.Path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/rdap+json\")\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n\t\t\t_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{\n\t\t\t\t\"objectClassName\": \"domain\",\n\t\t\t\t\"ldhName\": \"example.com\",\n\t\t\t\t\"entities\": [{\"ldhName\": \"Test Registrar\", \"roles\": [\"registrar\"]}],\n\t\t\t\t\"events\": [\n\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"registration\", \"eventDate\": \"2020-01-01T00:00:00Z\"},\n\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"expiration\", \"eventDate\": \"2025-01-01T00:00:00Z\"}\n\t\t\t\t]\n\t\t\t}`))\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"example.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif result.Available {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected domain to be registered\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Registration == nil || result.Registration.Registrar != \"Test Registrar\" {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected registrar, got: %+v\", result.Registration)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"available domain\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"available123.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !result.Available {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected domain to be available\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Registration != nil && result.Registration.Registrar != \"\" {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected no registrar for available domain, got: %s\", result.Registration.Registrar)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"rate limited\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusTooManyRequests)\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"ratelimited.com\")\n\t\tif err
